extract_sign      = extract_is_signed & shift_result[bmask_a_i];\n\n  assign bextins_result    = (bmask & shift_result) | (bextins_and & bmask_inv);\n\n  assign bclr_result       = operand_a_i & bmask_inv;\n  assign bset_result       = operand_a_i | bmask;\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Bit reverse\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n  logic [31:0] radix_2_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_4_rev;\n  logic [31:0] radix_8_rev;\n  logic [31:0] reverse_result;\n  logic [ 1:0] radix_mux_sel;\n\n  assign radix_mux_sel = bmask_a_i[1:0];\n\n  generate\n    genvar rb;\n    for (rb = 0; rb < 32; rb++) begin : gen_radix_2_rev\n      assign radix_2_rev[rb] = shift_result[31-rb];\n    end\n    for (rb = 0; rb < 16; rb++) begin : gen_radix_4_rev\n      assign radix_4_rev[2*rb+1:2*rb] = shift_result[31-rb*2:31-rb*2-1];\n    end\n    for (rb = 0; rb < 10; rb++) begin : gen_radix_8_rev\n      assign radix_8_rev[3*rb+2:3*rb] = shift_result[31-rb*3:31-rb*3-2];\n    end\n    assign radix_8_rev[31:30] = 2'b0;\n  endgenerate\n\n  always_comb begin\n    reverse_result = '0;\n\n    unique case (radix_mux_sel)\n      2'b00: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n      2'b01: reverse_result = radix_4_rev;\n      2'b10: reverse_result = radix_8_rev;\n\n      default: reverse_result = radix_2_rev;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Divider\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n  logic [31:0] result_div;\n  logic        div_ready;\n  logic        div_signed;\n  logic        div_op_a_signed;\n  logic [ 5:0] div_shift_int;\n\n  assign div_signed = operator_i[0];\n\n  assign div_op_a_signed = operand_a_i[31] & div_signed;\n\n  assign div_shift_int = ff_no_one ? 6'd31 : clb_result;\n  assign div_shift = div_shift_int + (div_op_a_signed ? 6'd0 : 6'd1);\n\n  assign div_valid = enable_i & ((operator_i == ALU_DIV) || (operator_i == ALU_DIVU) ||\n                     (operator_i == ALU_REM) || (operator_i == ALU_REMU));\n\n  cv32e40p_alu_div alu_div_i (\n      .Clk_CI (clk),\n      .Rst_RBI(rst_n),\n\n      .OpA_DI      (operand_b_i),\n      .OpB_DI      (shift_left_result),\n      .OpBShift_DI (div_shift),\n      .OpBIsZero_SI((cnt_result == 0)),\n\n      .OpBSign_SI(div_op_a_signed),\n      .OpCode_SI (operator_i[1:0]),\n\n      .Res_DO(result_div),\n\n      .InVld_SI (div_valid),\n      .OutRdy_SI(ex_ready_i),\n      .OutVld_SO(div_ready)\n  );\n\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n  // Result mux\n  //////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n\n  always_comb begin\n    result_o = '0;\n\n    unique case (operator_i)\n      ALU_AND: result_o = operand_a_i & operand_b_i;\n      ALU_OR:  result_o = operand_a_i | operand_b_i;\n      ALU_XOR: result_o = operand_a_i ^ operand_b_i;\n\n      ALU_ADD, ALU_ADDR, ALU_ADDU, ALU_ADDUR,\n      ALU_SUB, ALU_SUBR, ALU_SUBU, ALU_SUBUR,\n      ALU_SLL,\n      ALU_SRL, ALU_SRA,\n      ALU_ROR:\n      result_o = shift_result;\n\n      ALU_BINS, ALU_BEXT, ALU_BEXTU: result_o = bextins_result;\n\n      ALU_BCLR: result_o = bclr_result;\n      ALU_BSET: result_o = bset_result;\n\n      ALU_BREV: result_o = reverse_result;\n\n      ALU_SHUF, ALU_SHUF2, ALU_PCKLO, ALU_PCKHI, ALU_EXT, ALU_EXTS, ALU_INS: result_o = pack_result;\n\n      ALU_MIN, ALU_MINU, ALU_MAX, ALU_MAXU: result_o = result_minmax;\n\n      ALU_ABS: result_o = is_clpx_i ? {adder_result[31:16], operand_a_i[15:0]} : result_minmax;\n\n      ALU_CLIP, ALU_CLIPU: result_o = clip_result;\n\n      ALU_EQ, ALU_NE, ALU_GTU, ALU_GEU, ALU_LTU, ALU_LEU, ALU_GTS, ALU_GES, ALU_LTS, ALU_LES: begin\n        result_o[31:24] = {8{cmp_result[3]}};\n        result_o[23:16] = {8{cmp_result[2]}};\n        result_o[15:8]  = {8{cmp_result[1]}};\n        result_o[7:0]   = {8{cmp_result[0]}};\n      end\n\n      ALU_SLTS, ALU_SLTU, ALU_SLETS, ALU_SLETU: result_o = {31'b0, comparison_result_o};\n\n      ALU_FF1, ALU_FL1, ALU_CLB, ALU_CNT: result_o = {26'h0, bitop_result[5:0]};\n\n      ALU_DIV, ALU_DIVU, ALU_REM, ALU_REMU: result_o = result_div;\n\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign ready_o = div_ready;\n\nendmodule\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Parallel prefix adder (Sklansky-style)\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\nmodule alu_prefix_add #(\n    parameter int N = 32\n) (\n    input  logic [N-1:0] a_i,\n    input  logic [N-1:0] b_i,\n    input  logic         cin_i,\n    output logic [N-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic         co_o\n);\n  localparam int L = (N > 1) ? $clog2(N) : 1;\n  localparam int P = (N > 1) ? (1 << L) : 1;\n\n  logic [P-1:0] a, b, x;\n  logic [L:0][P-1:0] G, Pt;\n\n  assign a = {{(P-N){1'b0}}, a_i};\n  assign b = {{(P-N){1'b0}}, b_i};\n  assign x = a ^ b;\n\n  always_comb begin\n    G[0]  = a & b;\n    Pt[0] = a | b;\n    G[0][0] = (a[0] & b[0]) | ((a[0] | b[0]) & cin_i);\n  end\n\n  genvar lv, kk, ii;\n  generate\n    for (lv = 1; lv <= L; lv++) begin : gen_lvl\n      for (kk = 0; kk < (P >> lv); kk++) begin : gen_grp\n        for (ii = 0; ii < (1 << (lv-1)); ii++) begin : gen_bit\n          assign G[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+ii]  = G[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+ii];\n          assign Pt[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+ii] = Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+ii];\n          assign G[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii] =\n              G[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii]\n            | (Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii]\n               & G[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))-1]);\n          assign Pt[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii] =\n              Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii]\n            & Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))-1];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign s_o[0] = x[0] ^ cin_i;\n  generate\n    genvar gi;\n    for (gi = 1; gi < N; gi++) begin : gen_sum\n      assign s_o[gi] = x[gi] ^ G[L][gi-1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign co_o = G[L][N-1];\n\nendmodule\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Popcount (upstream)\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\nmodule cv32e40p_popcnt (\n    input  logic [31:0] in_i,\n    output logic [ 5:0] result_o\n);\n\n  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;\n  logic [ 7:0][2:0] cnt_l2;\n  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;\n  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;\n\n  genvar l, m, n, p;\n  generate\n    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1\n      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2\n      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3\n      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    for (p = 0; p < 2; p++) begin : gen_cnt_l4\n      assign cnt_l4[p] = {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p]} + {1'b0, cnt_l3[2*p+1]};\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign result_o = {1'b0, cnt_l4[0]} + {1'b0, cnt_l4[1]};\n\nendmodule\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Find first one (upstream)\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\nmodule cv32e40p_ff_one #(\n    parameter LEN = 32\n) (\n    input logic [LEN-1:0] in_i,\n\n    output logic [$clog2(LEN)-1:0] first_one_o,\n    output logic                   no_ones_o\n);\n\n  localparam NUM_LEVELS = $clog2(LEN);\n\n  logic [          LEN-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_lut;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0]                 sel_nodes;\n  logic [2**NUM_LEVELS-1:0][NUM_LEVELS-1:0] index_nodes;\n\n  generate\n    genvar j;\n    for (j = 0; j < LEN; j++) begin : gen_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[j] = $unsigned(j);\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  generate\n    genvar k;\n    genvar l;\n    genvar level;\n\n    assign sel_nodes[2**NUM_LEVELS-1] = 1'b0;\n\n    for (level = 0; level < NUM_LEVELS; level++) begin : gen_tree\n      if (level < NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_non_root_level\n        for (l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : gen_node\n          assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+l]   = sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] | sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n          assign index_nodes[2**level-1+l] = (sel_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] == 1'b1) ?\n                                           index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2] : index_nodes[2**(level+1)-1+l*2+1];\n        end\n      end\n      if (level == NUM_LEVELS - 1) begin : gen_root_level\n        for (k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : gen_node\n          if (k * 2 < LEN - 1) begin : gen_two\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k] = in_i[k*2] | in_i[k*2+1];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = (in_i[k*2] == 1'b1) ? index_lut[k*2] : index_lut[k*2+1];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 == LEN - 1) begin : gen_one\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = in_i[k*2];\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = index_lut[k*2];\n          end\n          if (k * 2 > LEN - 1) begin : gen_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2**level-1+k]   = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[2**level-1+k] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign first_one_o = index_nodes[0];\n  assign no_ones_o   = ~sel_nodes[0];\n\nendmodule\n\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\n// Serial divider (upstream structure, prefix adder + subtractor compare)\n////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////\nmodule cv32e40p_alu_div #(\n    parameter C_WIDTH     = 32,\n    parameter C_LOG_WIDTH = 6\n) (\n    input  logic                   Clk_CI,\n    input  logic                   Rst_RBI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpA_DI,\n    input  logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] OpB_DI,\n    input  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] OpBShift_DI,\n    input  logic                   OpBIsZero_SI,\n    input  logic                   OpBSign_SI,\n    input  logic [            1:0] OpCode_SI,\n    input  logic                   InVld_SI,\n    input  logic                   OutRdy_SI,\n    output logic                   OutVld_SO,\n    output logic [    C_WIDTH-1:0] Res_DO\n);\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP, ResReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] ResReg_DP_rev;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AReg_DP, AReg_DN;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BReg_DP, BReg_DN;\n\n  logic RemSel_SN, RemSel_SP;\n  logic CompInv_SN, CompInv_SP;\n  logic ResInv_SN, ResInv_SP;\n\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddOut_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] AddTmp_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] BMux_D;\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] OutMux_D;\n\n  logic [C_LOG_WIDTH-1:0] Cnt_DP, Cnt_DN;\n  logic CntZero_S;\n\n  logic ARegEn_S, BRegEn_S, ResRegEn_S, ABComp_S, PmSel_S, LoadEn_S;\n\n  enum logic [1:0] {\n    IDLE,\n    DIVIDE,\n    FINISH\n  }\n      State_SN, State_SP;\n\n  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpA_DI : BReg_DP;\n\n  assign BMux_D   = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // main comparator via subtractor: {ge, diff} = AReg - BReg (unsigned)\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] cmp_diff;\n  logic               cmp_ge;\n  logic               cmp_eq;\n  alu_prefix_add #(.N(32)) div_cmp_i (\n      .a_i  (AReg_DP),\n      .b_i  (~BReg_DP),\n      .cin_i(1'b1),\n      .s_o  (cmp_diff),\n      .co_o (cmp_ge)\n  );\n  assign cmp_eq  = ~(|cmp_diff);\n  assign ABComp_S = ((cmp_eq) | (((cmp_ge & ~cmp_eq)) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  // main adder: AddTmp + AddMux (PmSel) or AddTmp - AddMux (~PmSel)\n  logic [C_WIDTH-1:0] add_op;\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign add_op   = PmSel_S ? OpBShift_DI : (~CntZero_S) ? Cnt_DP - 1 : Cnt_DP;\n\n  assign CntZero_S = ~(|Cnt_DP);\n\n  always_comb begin : p_fsm\n    State_SN   = State_SP;\n\n    OutVld_SO  = 1'b0;\n\n    LoadEn_S   = 1'b0;\n\n    ARegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    BRegEn_S   = 1'b0;\n    ResRegEn_S = 1'b0;\n\n    case (State_SP)\n      IDLE: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n\n        if (InVld_SI) begin\n          OutVld_SO = 1'b0;\n          ARegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          BRegEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          LoadEn_S  = 1'b1;\n          State_SN  = DIVIDE;\n        end\n      end\n      DIVIDE: begin\n        ARegEn_S   = ABComp_S;\n        BRegEn_S   = 1'b1;\n        ResRegEn_S = 1'b1;\n\n        if (CntZero_S) begin\n          State_SN = FINISH;\n        end\n      end\n      FINISH: begin\n        OutVld_SO = 1'b1;\n\n        if (OutRdy_SI) begin\n          State_SN = IDLE;\n        end\n      end\n      default: ;\n    endcase\n  end\n\n  assign RemSel_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpCode_SI[1] : RemSel_SP;\n  assign CompInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? OpBSign_SI : CompInv_SP;\n  assign ResInv_SN = (LoadEn_S) ? (~OpBIsZero_SI | OpCode_SI[1]) & OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(\n      OpA_DI\n  )] ^ OpBSign_SI) : ResInv_SP;\n\n  assign AReg_DN = (ARegEn_S) ? AddOut_D : AReg_DP;\n  assign BReg_DN = (BRegEn_S) ? BMux_D : BReg_DP;\n  assign ResReg_DN = (LoadEn_S) ? '0 : (ResRegEn_S) ? {\n    ABComp_S, ResReg_DP[$high(ResReg_DP):1]\n  } : ResReg_DP;\n\n  always_ff @(posedge Clk_CI or negedge Rst_RBI) begin : p_regs\n    if (~Rst_RBI) begin\n      State_SP   <= IDLE;\n      AReg_DP    <= '0;\n      BReg_DP    <= '0;\n      ResReg_DP  <= '0;\n      Cnt_DP     <= '0;\n      RemSel_SP  <= 1'b0;\n      CompInv_SP <= 1'b0;\n      ResInv_SP  <= 1'b0;\n    end else begin\n      State_SP   <= State_SN;\n      AReg_DP    <= AReg_DN;\n      BReg_DP    <= BReg_DN;\n      ResReg_DP  <= ResReg_DN;\n      Cnt_DP     <= Cnt_DN;\n      RemSel_SP  <= RemSel_SN;\n      CompInv_SP <= CompInv_SN;\n      ResInv_SP  <= ResInv_SN;\n    end\n  end\n\nendmodule\n")

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// Parallel prefix adder (Sklansky-style)
////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
module alu_prefix_add #(
    parameter int N = 32
) (
    input  logic [N-1:0] a_i,
    input  logic [N-1:0] b_i,
    input  logic         cin_i,
    output logic [N-1:0] s_o,
    output logic         co_o
);
  localparam int L = (N > 1) ? $clog2(N) : 1;
  localparam int P = (N > 1) ? (1 << L) : 1;

  logic [P-1:0] a, b, x;
  logic [L:0][P-1:0] G, Pt;

  assign a = {{(P-N){1'b0}}, a_i};
  assign b = {{(P-N){1'b0}}, b_i};
  assign x = a ^ b;

  always_comb begin
    G[0]  = a & b;
    Pt[0] = a | b;
    G[0][0] = (a[0] & b[0]) | ((a[0] | b[0]) & cin_i);
  end

  genvar lv, kk, ii;
  generate
    for (lv = 1; lv <= L; lv++) begin : gen_lvl
      for (kk = 0; kk < (P >> lv); kk++) begin : gen_grp

[ASSISTANT]
My design now beats the original baseline (620213 vs 645629). The critical path: opcode decode → 36-bit adder → round adder → shared shifter (with input/amount muxes) → result mux. 

**Key optimization idea**: split the adder-family datapath out of the shared shifter. The adder family uses the shifter only for a per-lane right shift by `bmask_b_i`/`clpx_shift_i` — give it a dedicated lane shifter fed directly by the round adder, and strip the `shift_use_round` muxes out of the shared shifter.

[ASSISTANT]
Found it — the classic Verilog pitfall: in a ternary, the unsigned branch makes the whole expression unsigned, so `>>>` degenerates to `>>`. Let me rewrite using upstream's technique (`{arith & msb, lane} >>> amt`):


  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', old_string='  always_comb begin\n    case (vector_mode_i)\n      VEC_MODE16: begin\n        adder_shift_result[31:16] = adder_shift_arith ?\n module alu_prefix_add #(
    parameter int N = 32
) (
    input  logic [N-1:0] a_i,
    input  logic [N-1:0] b_i,
    input  logic         cin_i,
    output logic [N-1:0] s_o,
    output logic         co_o
);
  localparam int L = (N > 1) ? $clog2(N) : 1;
  localparam int P = (N > 1) ? (1 << L) : 1;

  logic [P-1:0] a, b, x;
  logic [L:0][P-1:0] G, Pt;

  assign a = {{(P-N){1'b0}}, a_i};
  assign b = {{(P-N){1'b0}}, b_i};
  assign x = a ^ b;

  always_comb begin
    G[0]  = a & b;
    Pt[0] = a | b;
    G[0][0] = (a[0] & b[0]) | ((a[0] | b[0]) & cin_i);
  end

  genvar lv, kk, ii;
  generate
    for (lv = 1; lv <= L; lv++) begin : gen_lvl
      for (kk = 0; kk < (P >> lv); kk++) begin : gen_grp
        for (ii = 0; ii < (1 << (lv-1)); ii++) begin : gen_bit
          assign G[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+ii]  = G[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+ii];
          assign Pt[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+ii] = Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+ii];
          assign G[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii] =
              G[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii]
            | (Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii]
               & G[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))-1]);
          assign Pt[lv][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii] =
              Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))+ii]
            & Pt[lv-1][kk*(1<<lv)+(1<<(lv-1))-1];
        end
      end
    end
  endgenerate

  assign s_o[0] = x[0] ^ cin_i;
  generate
    genvar gi;
    for (gi = 1; gi < N; gi++) begin : gen_sum
      assign s_o[gi] = x[gi] ^ G[L][gi-1];
    end
  endgenerate
  assign co_o = G[L][N-1];

endmodule

////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////////
// Popcount (upstream)

module cv32e40p_popcnt (
    input  logic [31:0] in_i,
    output logic [ 5:0] result_o
);

  logic [15:0][1:0] cnt_l1;

  logic [ 3:0][3:0] cnt_l3;
  logic [ 1:0][4:0] cnt_l4;

    for (l = 0; l < 16; l++) begin : gen_cnt_l1
      assign cnt_l1[l] = {1'b0, in_i[2*l]} + {1'b0, in_i[2*l+1]};
    end
  endgenerate

  generate
    for (m = 0; m < 8; m++) begin : gen_cnt_l2
      assign cnt_l2[m] = {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m]} + {1'b0, cnt_l1[2*m+1]};
    end
  endgenerate

  generate
    for (n = 0; n < 4; n++) begin : gen_cnt_l3
      assign cnt_l3[n] = {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n]} + {1'b0, cnt_l2[2*n+1]};
    end

$clog2(N) : 1;\n\n  logic [L:0][N-1:0] G, P;\n\n  assign G[0] = a_i & b_i;\n  assign P[0] = a_i ^ b_i;\n\n  genvar lv, i;\n  generate\n    for (lv = 1; lv <= L; lv++) begin : gen_lvl\n      for (i = 0; i < N; i++) begin : gen_bit\n        if (i >= (1 << (lv-1))) begin : gen_hi\n          assign G[lv][i] = G[lv-1][i] | (P[lv-1][i] & G[lv-1][i-(1<<(lv-1))]);\n          assign P[lv][i] = P[lv-1][i] & P[lv-1][i-(1<<(lv-1))];\n        end else begin : gen_lo\n          assign G[lv][i] = G[lv-1][i];\n          assign P[lv][i] = P[lv-1][i];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n    assign s_o[0] = P[0][0];\n    for (i = 1; i < N; i++) begin : gen_sum\n      assign s_o[i] = P[0][i] ^ G[L][i-1];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n  assign co_o = G[L][N-1];\n\nendmodule')

Now let me optimize the divider datapath for area: during the DIVIDE state, the main adder already computes `AReg - BReg` (since PmSel=0), so the separate 32-bit `==` and `>` comparators are redundant — I can derive the compare from the subtractor's carry-out and zero flag. This removes two comparator trees (~significant area). Let me implement carefully:


  -> edit_file(filename='design.sv', old_string='  assign PmSel_S  = LoadEn_S & ~(OpCode_SI[0] & (OpA_DI[$high(OpA_DI)] ^ OpBSign_SI));\n\n  assign AddMux_D = (LoadEn_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? AReg_DP : ResReg_DP_rev;\n\n  assign Res_DO = (ResInv_SP) ? -$signed(OutMux_D) : OutMux_D;\n\n  // main comparator\n  assign ABComp_S    = ((AReg_DP == BReg_DP) | ((AReg_DP > BReg_DP) ^ CompInv_SP)) & ((|AReg_DP) | OpBIsZero_SI);\n\n  // main adder\n  assign AddTmp_D = (LoadEn_S) ? 0 : AReg_DP;\n  assign AddOut_D = (PmSel_S) ? OpB_DI : {CompInv_SP, (BReg_DP[$high(BReg_DP):1])};\n\n  genvar index;\n  generate\n    for (index = 0; index < C_WIDTH; index++) begin : gen_bit_swapping\n      assign ResReg_DP_rev[index] = ResReg_DP[C_WIDTH-1-index];\n    end\n  endgenerate\n\n  assign OutMux_D = (RemSel_SP) ? ## Session Summary — cv32e40p_alu optimization

**Final: PASS, area_delay_product = 531,024** (baseline 645,629 → −18%). Final design is `design.sv` (the last edit restored exactly the eval-10 content, so carried-over best is consistent).

### 1. Approaches tried
- **Exact-width Kogge-Stone adders** (`ks_add` module in `design.sv`) for the two chained critical adders — the winner.
- Hand Sklansky prefix adders with power-of-2 padding (worse), dedicated adder-family post-shifter (worse), MSB-flip unsigned-compare trick (worse), divider subtractor/comparator sharing (worse).
- `target_delay` sweep (250/500/1500 ps): no effect — the design is pure combinational depth-limited; yosys maps identically.

### 2. Most impactful optimization
Replacing `$signed(adder_in_a) + $signed(adder_in_b)` (36-bit) and `adder_result + adder_round_value` (32-bit) with exact-width Kogge-Stone trees: **delay 1414→1174 ps, cost 620k→531k**. The native `+` compiled to ~68 ripple-carry stages in series (MAJ/XOR chains dominated the timing report). Key details: XOR-propagate (`P = a^b`) makes the final sum XOR free, no width padding, and bit 36 reconstructed as `co ^ a[35] ^ b[35]` to match the upstream 37-bit signed sum sign.

### 3. Regressions / failures
- **Padded prefix adder** (36→64 bits, `|`-propagate): 668k vs 645k — padding doubles prefix levels and hurts area+delay.
- **Missing driver** (`cmp_eq`) after an edit → 1000 sim errors; always verify every signal stays assigned.
- **6-bit context semantics**: upstream `clb_result = ff1_result - 5'd1` computed in 6 bits makes `div_shift` wrap to 0 when the divisor MSB is at bit 31; a 5-bit wrap broke the divider timing/values.
- **Verilog ternary unsigned contagion**: `(arith ? $signed(x)>>>n : x>>n)` degenerates `>>>` to `>>`; use upstream's `{arith & msb, lane} >>> amt` pattern instead.
- **yosys already shares logic**: the divider comparator-share and MSB-flip compare both added muxing and lost (542k/559k vs 531k).

### 4. Lessons / next steps
- On this flow, delay is the dominant lever (cost = area × true path delay); spending area on log-depth arithmetic pays off.
- Critical path is now opcode-decode → compare/minmax/shift logic (~1174 ps). Promising next moves: prefix-carry based `A>=B` for `is_greater_vec` (done with a proper shared prefix structure, not RTL-level tricks), Brent-Kung for the round adder to reclaim area at equal depth, and `ks_add` in the divider datapath. Also try re-ordering the result mux (biggest cell delays were near the output mux) — structural changes that move mux selects earlier tend to help yosys.
- Process: change one thing, evaluate, revert fast; keep the known-good file intact (`design.sv` at eval-10 state = best).
